### [73] [AgroVisNet: A lightweight Convolutional Network and the BD-PlantDX Expert-Validated Benchmark for Radish, Potato and Pointed Gourd Disease Classification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.10469)
*Md. Abdullah Mandal,Saad Ahmed,Md. Khalid Syfullah*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: AgroVisNet是一个紧凑的卷积网络，与专家验证的BD-PlantDX基准数据集一起用于植物病害诊断。该模型仅需290,572个可训练参数，在12,432张图像的测试集上达到99.52%的准确率和加权F1分数，优于6个ImageNet预训练的轻量级骨干网络。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 三个障碍限制了植物病害自动诊断的实际价值：1）公共基准数据集以非本地作物为主；2）区域特定数据集很少经过领域专家验证；3）达到竞争性准确率的架构参数预算过大，不适合低成本硬件。

Method: 提出AgroVisNet紧凑卷积网络，包含：分组瓶颈残差块（带顺序通道和空间注意力）、多尺度深度块和双池化分类头。同时构建BD-PlantDX专家验证基准数据集，包含12,432张孟加拉国Bogura和Nilphamari地区的田间图像，涵盖12种类别（萝卜、土豆和苦瓜的健康与病害状态）。

Result: 在BD-PlantDX上达到99.52%测试准确率和99.52%加权F1，超过6个ImageNet预训练轻量级骨干网络，参数减少8.7-16.8倍，MAC减少1.3-8.5倍。量化后为0.46MB全整数网络，精度仅损失0.22个百分点。单CPU推理时间8.40ms。五次随机种子准确率99.57±0.10%。迁移到两个独立数据集分别达到98.71%和99.05%准确率。Grad-CAM显示预测基于病斑叶片区域。

Conclusion: AgroVisNet在极低参数量的条件下实现了高精度植物病害诊断，具有良好的泛化能力和部署可行性，为资源受限的农业地区提供了实用的解决方案。

Abstract: Automated plant disease diagnosis is increasingly deployed on farmer-held devices in regions where agronomic expertise is scarce and network connectivity is unreliable. Three obstacles limit its practical value: public benchmarks are dominated by a small set of non-native crops, region-specific datasets are rarely validated by domain experts, and the architectures that reach competitive accuracy carry parameter budgets that are unsuited to low-cost hardware. We propose AgroVisNet, a compact convolutional network trained from scratch, together with BD-PlantDX, an expert-validated benchmark of 12,432 field images spanning 12 classes of radish, potato and pointed gourd in healthy and diseased states, collected across the Bogura and Nilphamari districts of Bangladesh. AgroVisNet couples grouped bottleneck residual blocks carrying sequential channel and spatial attention with multi-scale depthwise blocks and a dual-pooling classification head, reaching 290,572 trainable parameters. On BD-PlantDX the model attains 99.52% test accuracy and 99.52% weighted F1, exceeding all six ImageNet-pretrained lightweight backbones evaluated under an identical protocol while using 8.7 to 16.8 times fewer parameters and 1.3 to 8.5 times fewer multiply-accumulate operations. Exported for deployment, the model quantises to a 0.46 MB full-integer network at a 0.22 percentage-point accuracy cost and classifies an image in 8.40 ms on a single CPU. Across five random seeds accuracy remains at 99.57 +- 0.10%, a ten-variant ablation isolates the contribution of each component, and the same architecture transfers without redesign to two independently collected datasets at 98.71% and 99.05% accuracy. Grad-CAM evidence indicates that predictions rest on lesion-bearing leaf regions rather than on background cues.

</details>


### [74] [Artificial Intelligence Literacy and Sustainable Development: An Ethical Governance and Development Goals Framework](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.10489)
*Md. Masudul Islam,Mirza Niaz Morshed,Md. Shafiqul Islam*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 该研究将AI素养定位为治理能力，提出六层AI推理与伦理_taxonomy_，通过300名专业人员的调查揭示技术意识强但伦理与治理准备不足的现状，强调伦理推理是可持续可信AI使用的最强预测因素，呼吁将素养能力嵌入教育、治理机制以推进SDGs。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前AI技术快速发展，但公众在伦理推理和治理准备方面存在显著差距，需要建立AI素养作为治理能力来支撑17个可持续发展目标的实现。

Method: 构建六层AI推理与伦理_taxonomy_（扩展传统学习模型纳入伦理判断和战略远见），对300名来自不同专业背景的参与者开展问卷调查，采用定量分析方法。

Result: 调查揭示强烈的技术意识但有限的伦理与治理准备度，伦理推理和反思性思维是可持续可信AI使用的最强预测因素。

Conclusion: 需将基于素养的能力嵌入课程、机构政策和治理机制，以加速实现可持续发展目标的公平和负责任进展。

Abstract: AI literacy provides foundational competencies that support ethical, transparent, and sustainable technological development, although higher-order capabilities such as governance, critical evaluation, and strategic decision-making extend beyond basic literacy into advanced levels of AI competency. This study positions AI literacy as a governance capacity that complements and strengthens all 17 SDGs. It introduces a six-level taxonomy of artificial intelligence reasoning and ethics that extends traditional learning models by incorporating ethical judgement and strategic foresight. This taxonomy forms the foundation of an integrated framework linking education, governance, and sustainable development. A survey of 300 participants from diverse professional backgrounds within a national context which reveals strong technical awareness but limited ethical and governance readiness, highlighting critical gaps in public capacity to manage artificial intelligence responsibly. Findings show that ethical reasoning and reflective thinking are the strongest predictors of sustainable and trustworthy artificial intelligence use. The study proposed to embed literacy-based competencies into curricula, institutional policies, and governance mechanisms to accelerate equitable and responsible progress toward sustainable development goals

</details>


### [75] [Cross-Model Agreement as a Deployment-Time Reliability Signal for Automatic Polyp Segmentation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.10495)
*Siddharth Gupta,Jitin Singla*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出Referee-Based Quality Estimation (RBQE)，一种无参考框架，通过测量主分割模型与独立训练的裁判模型在同一图像上的一致性来估计分割质量。在1,223张外部图像基准上，跨架构裁判模型SegFormer-B0获得最佳性能（ROC-AUC = 0.960），显著优于相同架构控制和UNet++，并超过TTA基线0.055 ROC-AUC。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 实时结肠镜检查中无法获得真实标注，导致息肉分割模型可能静默失败。需要一种无参考的质量估计方法来识别不可靠的预测。

Method: RBQE通过主分割模型与独立训练的裁判模型之间的Agreement Dice一致性来估计质量。实验采用四种裁判配置，涵盖两个设计轴：裁判独立性和架构多样性。

Result: 相同架构但不同随机初始化的裁判已产生有用的可靠性信号（ROC-AUC = 0.923）。跨架构裁判表现更优：SegFormer-B0达到最佳（ROC-AUC = 0.960），显著优于相同架构控制和UNet++，超过TTA基线0.055 AUC。排除空掩码后，SegFormer-B0在1,046张图像上ROC-AUC降至0.876。RBQE通过逐步拒绝低一致性案例来提高保留预测的Dice均值。

Conclusion: 跨模型一致性可作为自动息肉分割的实用、可解释可靠性框架。仅需一次额外的确定性裁判前向传递即可实现推理。SegFormer-B0跨架构裁判表现最佳。

Abstract: In real-time colonoscopy, ground-truth annotations are unavailable at inference, so polyp segmentation models can fail silently. We propose Referee-Based Quality Estimation (RBQE), a reference-free framework measuring agreement between a primary segmentation model and an independently trained referee on the same image. RBQE is evaluated on a standardized 1,223-image external benchmark drawn from four public datasets, using four referee configurations chosen to separate two design axes: referee independence and architectural diversity. Using a common Agreement Dice descriptor, a same-architecture referee differing from the primary model only in random initialization already yields a useful reliability signal (ROC-AUC = 0.923), showing that independent training alone is sufficient. Cross-architecture referees improve further: SegFormer-B0 achieves the strongest performance (ROC-AUC = 0.960), significantly outperforming the same-architecture control and UNet++, and exceeding a representative Test-Time Augmentation baseline by 0.055 ROC-AUC under an identical protocol, whereas a prompt-coupled MedSAM referee underperforms despite maximal architectural diversity. Because empty-mask agreement is trivially separable, we also report a restricted evaluation excluding such cases: ROC-AUC falls to 0.876 (SegFormer-B0, 1,046 images) and 0.783 (same-architecture control, 975 images), yet RBQE's margin over both baselines widens on this identical subset. RBQE additionally increases the mean Dice of retained predictions as low-agreement cases are progressively rejected, supporting selective prediction, and requires only one additional deterministic referee forward pass at inference. Our study therefore supports cross-model agreement as a practical, interpretable reliability framework for automated polyp segmentation.

</details>


### [76] [Field Converter: Geometry-Initialized Temporal Residual Refinement for World-Grounded Player Pose Estimation from Soccer Broadcasts](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.10498)
*Simon Khan,Laurent Gajny,Jennyfer Lecompte,Sébastien Laporte*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: Field Converter是一个用于从标定足球转播中估计世界坐标3D球员姿态的几何初始化时间残差框架，通过几何初始化和时序残差校正将根误差从49cm降低到10-11cm，世界空间MPJPE达到13.2cm。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 从单目体育转播中恢复3D人体姿态具有挑战性，特别是当球员需要在共享的度量世界坐标系中定位，而不仅仅是相对于他们自己的身体进行重建。

Method: 使用相机和场地几何通过射线-地面交点初始化球员根部，然后从姿态、图像、相机和几何线索预测时间残差校正。使用MLP、TCN和Transformer三种不同的时序 backbone 进行实验。

Result: 残差细化将根部误差从几何 alone 的49cm降低到：MLP的14cm，TCN的10cm，Transformer的11cm。世界空间MPJPE达到13.2cm。消融实验表明残差预测明显优于直接全局根部回归，时序上下文比特定时序 backbone 更重要。

Conclusion: 时间残差校正方法有效解决了世界坐标 grounded 的3D球员姿态估计问题。空中运动是基于地面的几何初始化的主要限制。

Abstract: Recovering 3D human pose from monocular sports broadcasts remains challenging when players must be localized in a shared metric world coordinate system rather than only reconstructed relative to their own body. We introduce Field Converter, a geometry-initialized temporal residual framework for world-grounded 3D player pose estimation from calibrated soccer broadcasts. Our method first uses camera and pitch geometry to initialize the player root through ray-ground intersection, then predicts a temporal residual correction from pose, image, camera, and geometric cues. On match-disjoint evaluation sequences, residual refinement reduces root error from 49cm with geometry alone to 14cm with a frame-wise MLP and 10cm with a TCN, while a Transformer achieves a comparable 11cm. The resulting world-space MPJPE reaches 13.2cm, and ablations show that residual prediction clearly outperforms direct global-root regression while temporal context matters more than the specific temporal backbone. Failure analysis further identifies airborne motion as the main limitation of the ground-based geometric initialization.

</details>


### [77] [BrainTaskonomy: Learning How to Pretrain and What to Transfer in fMRI Foundation Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.10518)
*Junfeng Xia,Wenhao Ye,Junxiang Zhang,Jiayu Zuo,Mo Wang,Quanying Liu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文研究如何通过测量的学习关系来组织fMRI基础模型的预训练和适应阶段，而非将域和任务视为独立的平面集合。提出了Brain-DiT代理来估计难度和引导关系，采用优先级引导的课程学习和高到低噪声时间步调度，以及基于预算整数编程的任务选择方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前fMRI基础模型将异构数据聚合时，预训练域通常被简单地视为平面混合物，下游任务被独立适应。本文旨在通过测量的学习关系来组织这两个阶段，而不修改骨干网络。

Method: 预训练阶段：使用轻量级Brain-DiT代理估计10个fMRI域之间的难度和定向促进关系，生成优先级引导的累积域课程，结合高到低噪声时间步调度和联合整合。适应阶段：跨15个任务受控的一阶和高阶迁移构建定向任务onomy，从中通过预算整数编程(BIP)选择直接监督的源任务和目标特定路由。

Result: 与两个维度的均匀采样相比，优先级域和高到低时间步课程使v-NMSE降低6.5%，PSD-NMSE降低16.3%，FC-MSE降低10.5%，并在6个域内和域外任务中表现出强大的下游性能。任务onomy揭示了非对称的、目标依赖的迁移，而探索性封闭测试评估显示BIP策略在高阶路由空间可用时比匹配的随机对照获得更大的描述性增益。

Conclusion: 研究结果表明，应该通过测量的学习关系来组织fMRI预训练和适应，而非将域和任务视为独立的平面集合。

Abstract: fMRI foundation models increasingly aggregate heterogeneous data across brain states, cohorts, and acquisition settings, yet pretraining domains are commonly treated as a flat mixture and downstream tasks are adapted independently. We study whether measured learning relations can organize both stages without modifying the backbone. During pretraining, a lightweight Brain-DiT proxy estimates difficulty and directed facilitation across ten fMRI domains, yielding a priority-guided cumulative domain curriculum combined with high-to-low-noise timestep scheduling and joint consolidation. During adaptation, controlled first- and higher-order transfer across fifteen tasks constructs a directed taskonomy, from which budgeted integer programming (BIP) selects directly supervised source tasks and target-specific routes. The joint priority-domain and high-to-low-timestep curriculum reduces v-NMSE, PSD-NMSE, and FC-MSE by 6.5%, 16.3%, and 10.5%, respectively, relative to uniform sampling over both dimensions, and shows strong downstream performance across six in- and out-of-domain tasks. The taskonomy reveals asymmetric, target-dependent transfer, while exploratory sealed-test evaluation shows larger descriptive gains for BIP policies when higher-order route spaces are available than for matched random controls. Together, these findings support organizing fMRI pretraining and adaptation by measured learning relations rather than treating domains and tasks as independent flat sets.

</details>

### [79] [Guiding Image-to-3D Generation with Test-Time Partial Observations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.10531)
*Jerred Chen,Simon Weber,Ronald Clark*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出一种无需训练的测试时引导框架，将部分几何观测融入预训练的图像到3D生成模型中，通过射线一致的观测似然函数定义来改进几何保真度，无需重新训练或微调。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 图像到3D模型虽然能从单张RGB图像生成视觉效果良好的3D资产，但其几何形状往往仅松散地受限于可用观测，在需要几何保真度的应用中存在局限性。现实中测试时往往可以获得部分几何观测，但现有方法无法有效利用这些信息。

Method: 提出一种无需训练的框架，将几何证据融入预训练的图像到3D生成模型。具体方法：利用模型occupancy表示上定义的射线一致观测似然函数，引导生成过程，结合表面occupancy和自由空间证据。

Result: 应用于SAM 3D及其多视图扩展，显著提高了不同观测程度下的几何保真度和视觉质量。

Conclusion: 预训练的图像到3D模型可以通过显式的测试时引导有效整合部分几何观测，在不修改底层模型的情况下补充其学习到的生成先验。

Abstract: Image-to-3D models can generate visually compelling 3D assets from a single RGB image, but their geometry is often only loosely constrained by the available observations, limiting their use in applications that require geometric fidelity. In many real-world settings, however, partial geometric observations of the object may be available at test time. We introduce a training-free framework for incorporating such evidence into pretrained image-to-3D generative models without retraining or finetuning. To do this, we guide generation using a ray-consistent observation likelihood defined over the model's occupancy representation, combining surface occupancy and free-space evidence. Applied to SAM 3D and its multi-view extension, our approach substantially improves geometric fidelity across different levels of observability, as well as visual quality. Our results demonstrate that pretrained image-to-3D models can effectively integrate partial geometric observations through explicit test-time guidance, complementing their learned generative priors without modifying the underlying model.

</details>


### [80] [Programmable World Model](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.10540)
*Zheng-Hui Huang,Guixu Lin,Jiacheng Lin,Yi-Chuan Huang,Ruihan Yu,Muyao Niu,Siqi Yang,Yu-Lun Liu,Yung-Yu Chuang,Kaipeng Zhang,Zhixiang Wang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出Programmable World Model框架，将世界状态演变与视觉观察生成解耦，通过自然语言指令转换为可执行程序来控制实体状态和转换规则，并使用状态增强的3D定向边界盒作为中间表示连接到生成式渲染器，在CombatStateBench基准上实现了94%的计数准确率和98%的状态准确率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前视频世界模型虽然能生成越来越逼真和交互式的视觉体验，但缺乏维持持久世界状态和在对扩展交互执行可编程规则的可靠机制。

Method: 将自然语言指令翻译成可执行程序来指定实体状态和状态转换规则；使用轻量级引擎执行程序以更新和维护显式持久的世界状态；引入状态增强的3D定向边界盒(OBB)作为中间表示，将其与目标相机轨迹determin编译成用于预训练视频模型的像素对齐时空 conditioning 信号。

Result: 在CombatStateBench上达到94%计数准确率和98%状态准确率，显著优于现有交互式视频世界模型，同时支持连贯的长视野生成。

Conclusion: 将显式状态演变与生成式渲染分离对于构建持久、可编程的世界是有效的。

Abstract: Recent video world models generate increasingly realistic and interactive visual experiences, yet lack reliable mechanisms for maintaining persistent world state and enforcing programmable rules over extended interactions. We introduce Programmable World Model, a framework that decouples world-state evolution from visual observation generation. An agent translates natural-language instructions into executable programs that specify entity states and state-transition rules, enabling direct control over individual entities and their interactions. A lightweight engine executes these programs to update and maintain an explicit, persistent global world state, including off-screen entities and non-visual attributes. To connect world state with visual generation, we introduce state-augmented 3D oriented bounding boxes (OBBs) as an intermediate representation. This representation, together with the target camera trajectory, is deterministically compiled into pixel-aligned spatiotemporal conditioning signals for a pretrained video model serving as the generative renderer. This design allows users to create playable games with predefined mechanics, direct control over individual entities, and persistent world state throughout gameplay. We further introduce CombatStateBench, a benchmark for evaluating programmable world models. On CombatStateBench, our method achieves 94% Count Accuracy and 98% State Accuracy, substantially outperforming existing interactive video world models while supporting coherent long-horizon generation. These results demonstrate the effectiveness of separating explicit state evolution from generative rendering for building persistent, programmable worlds.

</details>

### [81] [The Hyperbolic Surface Distance, Diameter, and Dirichlet Problems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.09549)
*Vincent Despre,Auguste Gezalyan,Marc Pouget*

Main category: cs.CG

TL;DR: 本文解决了双曲曲面上基本算法问题的开放性难题。文章主要贡献包括：提出了计算两点间距离的O(g²)算法、解答固定源点距离查询的O(g² log g)方法、任意点为中心的Dirichlet域重center方法、O(g³ log g / ε²)的直径近似算法，以及直径的精确可计算性证明。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 双曲曲面在数学中具有重要地位，但长期以来关于双曲曲面的基本算法问题（如计算两点间的距离）一直未得到解决，原因是传统方法依赖于多面体结构，而光滑曲面缺乏这种结构。这使得双曲曲面的许多特性无法被访问和利用。

Method: 作者首先建立了双曲曲面的距离计算框架，开发了高效的算法来处理光滑双曲曲面（而非多面体）。核心方法包括：1）利用双曲几何的数学性质设计距离算法；2）通过迭代方法处理固定源点的距离查询；3）运用Dirichlet域的重心技术；4）使用近似算法逼近直径。

Result: 论文取得了以下成果：1）两点间距离计算算法复杂度为O(g²)；2）固定源点距离查询方法复杂度为O(g² log g)；3）可对任意点重center Dirichlet域；4）直径近似精度达到任意ε，复杂度为O(g³ log g / ε²)；5）证明直径的双曲余弦是定义曲面的双曲等距变换系数域上的代数数，从而直径可精确计算。

Conclusion: 该研究突破了双曲曲面算法研究的历史性障碍，使得这一类重要数学对象的计算分析成为可能。成果不仅在理论上有重要意义，也为双曲曲面的实际应用开辟了道路，其中直径作为一个编码大量曲面信息的单一实数，其精确可计算性具有特别重要的价值。

Abstract: Despite the prominence of hyperbolic surfaces in mathematics, basic algorithmic questions about them, even computing the distance between two points, have remained open, leaving many features of these surfaces inaccessible. The classical machinery assumes a polyhedral structure absent on a smooth surface. We remove these obstacles. We begin with an efficient $O(g^2)$ algorithm for the distance between two points, where $g$ is the genus of the surface. Building on it, we obtain an $O(g^2 \log g)$ method for answering distance queries from a fixed source and, as a consequence, for recentering a Dirichlet domain around an arbitrary point. This understanding of distances on the surface then lets us approximate the diameter to within any $\eps$ in time $O(g^3 \log g / \eps^2)$. We further show that the diameter, a single real number encoding a great deal about the surface, is exactly computable. Its hyperbolic cosine is an algebraic number over the field encoding the coefficients of the hyperbolic isometries defining the surface.

</details>


### [82] [A 2.37332-Competitive Algorithm for Online Square Packing with Gravity](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.10101)
*Nichlas Langhoff Rasmussen*

Main category: cs.CG

TL;DR: 研究在线将轴平行正方形打包到单位宽度带状区域的问题，提出名为AsymmetricSlots的递归算法，通过将每个槽分割为宽和窄子槽来改进竞争比至2.37332


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 之前算法竞争比为34/13≈2.6154，研究旨在设计竞争比更优的在线打包算法

Method: 提出基于分割槽为宽窄子槽的递归算法，使用局部充电论证：大正方形用自己的面积支付其创造的高度，小正方形在两个子槽之间平衡

Result: 对于合适分割参数p*，证明AsymmetricSlots算法竞争比不超过2.37332OPT+O(1)，同时对矩形给出O(κ)竞争比并证明下界Ω(κ)，正方形算法下界为2

Conclusion: 新算法显著改进了之前结果，框架也适用于矩形且依赖κ是渐近最优的

Abstract: We consider online packing of axis-parallel squares into a unit-width strip under the Tetris and gravity constraints: An incoming square must be lowered from above along a monotonic downwards path until it reaches support from below. Fekete, Kamphans, and Schweer [Algorithmica, 2014] gave an algorithm with asymptotic competitive ratio $34/13\approx2.6154$ in this model. We present $\mathrm{{AsymmetricSlots}}$, a recursive algorithm based on splitting each slot into a wide and narrow subslot. The proof uses a local charging argument: squares that are large relative to its associated slot pay for the height they create with their own area, while smaller squares are balanced between the two subslots and may use a bounded temporary credit. For a suitable split parameter $p^\star$, we prove $\mathrm{{AsymmetricSlots}}{{p^\star}}(σ)\le 2.37332 \operatorname{{OPT}} (σ)+O(1)$ for every input sequence $σ$. Additionally, we show that the same framework gives an algorithm with asymptotic competitive ratio $O(κ)$ for rectangles of aspect ratio at most $κ$, and a matching $ Ω(κ)$ lower bound shows that the dependence on $κ$ is asymptotically optimal. For the square algorithm, we give a lower bound of $2$ on its asymptotic competitive ratio.

</details>


### [83] [iLogMap: Geodesic Polar Coordinates Parameterization with the Magnetic Laplacian](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.10503)
*Tomás Banduc,Simone Pezzuto,Francisco Sahli Costabal*

Main category: cs.CG

TL;DR: iLogMap是一种在曲面上计算测地极坐标的方法，通过将测地距离 circumferential方向场的基态磁特征问题来重新表述对数映射的角分量。该方法可扩展到各向异性度量张量和实体体积，实现四面体网格中的圆柱和球参数化。实验表明该方法在角度准确性和度量失真方面具有竞争力，在有边界曲面和各向异性域上性能更优，并成功应用于计算心脏病学领域。动机：现有测地极坐标估计方法难以处理各向异性度量、高曲率和复杂拓扑结构。方法：将角分量转化为基态磁特征问题。结果：在各种形状上确认了竞争性的角度准确性和降低的度量失真。结论：iLogMap可有效初始化心房表面螺旋相并估计心室模型局部激活模式。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有测地极坐标(GPCs)精确估计具有挑战性，特别是在各向异性度量、高曲率和复杂拓扑结构存在的情况下。计算心脏病学应用需要准确的测地坐标来初始化螺旋相和估计激活模式。

Method: 将测地距离circumferential方向场的对数映射角分量重新表述为基态磁特征问题，可扩展到各向异性度量张量和实体体积，实现四面体网格中的圆柱和球参数化。

Result: 在具有不同亏格的各种形状上实验证实了竞争性的角度准确性和降低的度量失真，在有边界曲面和各向异性域上性能优于基于热量的方法，成功应用于心房表面螺旋相初始化和心室模型局部激活模式估计。

Conclusion: iLogMap方法能够准确计算各向异性度量和高曲率曲面及实体体积的测地极坐标，在计算心脏病学领域具有实用价值，可用于心律失常分析和心脏电生理建模。

Abstract: Geodesic polar coordinates (GPCs) provide an intrinsic parameterization over curved surfaces, but their accurate estimation remains challenging, particularly in the presence of anisotropic metrics, high curvature and complex topology. We introduce iLogMap, a method for computing GPCs in curved domains that recasts the angular component of the logarithmic map to a ground-state magnetic eigenproblem over the circumferential direction field of geodesic distance. Our method effortlessly extends to anisotropic metric tensors and solid volumes, enabling cylindrical and spherical parameterizations in tetrahedral meshes. Experiments on diverse shapes with varying genus confirm competitive angular accuracy and reduced metric distortion relative to heat-based methods, with improved performance on surfaces with boundary and domains with anisotropy. We demonstrate the utility of iLogMap in computational cardiology applications, where we use it to initialize spiral phases on atrial surfaces and estimate local activation patterns in ventricular models.

</details>


<div id='cs.AI'></div>

# cs.AI [[Back]](#toc)

### [84] [UnitBoost: Managing Compound LLM Systems with a Merge Operator, Not a Model](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.09815)
*Xing Zhang,Guanghui Wang,Yanwei Cui,Mengdie Flora Wang,Peiyang He*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 论文提出UnitBoost方法，用确定性元算子替代LLM系统中的生成式管理器，实现订单自由、单位溯源和可测试的失败条件。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 复合LLM系统中，管理层LLM将三个控制决策集中在一个不透明、顺序敏感的模型调用中，导致语义自由度虽高但缺乏可解释性和顺序不变性。

Method: UnitBoost用三个组件替代生成式管理器：1)任务给定的单位映射将worker输出转换为槽值提案；2)约束argmax组装输出；3)未填充或不支持的槽位成为下一轮的显式残差。

Result: 在三个保留基准测试中，比使用黄金标签选择的最佳单一候选高出0.060-0.195分，比输入匹配的生成式管理器高出0.048-0.076分。替换管理步骤使六种复合系统配置提升0.013-0.182。残差导向轮次将FanOutQA细胞F1从0.4778提升至0.5524。

Conclusion: 放弃语义自由换取顺序不变性、单位溯源和可测试的失败条件；识别了三种无法获益的条件并量化了跨单位耦合的修复成本。

Abstract: Compound LLM systems often solve a coordination problem by adding a higher-level LLM. The resulting meta-agent reads workers' outputs, writes the final answer, allocates later calls, and decides when to stop. It is expressive, but it also concentrates three control decisions in an opaque, order-sensitive model call. We ask whether the manager needs to be generative at all. UnitBoost replaces that model with a defined meta-level operator: a task-given unit map turns worker outputs into slot-value proposals, a constrained argmax assembles the output, and the slots left unfilled or unsupported become an explicit residual for the next round. The operator is order-free, records unit provenance, and gives a simple guarantee: without coupling constraints, unit-wise maximization under the same admission score dominates selection of any complete candidate. On three held-out benchmarks, it exceeds the best single candidate chosen with gold labels by 0.060-0.195 absolute task-score points and input-matched generative managers by 0.048-0.076. Replacing only the management step improves six compound-system configurations by 0.013-0.182. Residual-directed rounds raise FanOutQA cell F1 from 0.4778 to 0.5524; matched controls show that the true residual outperforms random targets and ordinary rereading, while a label-free supply signal flags exhaustion after one unproductive round. The same analysis measures three conditions in which no such gain is available (one indivisible unit, unavailable unit identity, and an endpoint that charges for every emitted unit) and quantifies cross-unit coupling as a repair cost. The manager gives up semantic freedom and gains order invariance, unit provenance, and testable failure conditions.

</details>


### [85] [OpenDiscoveryTrace: Process Traces for Evaluating AI Scientist Workflows](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.09203)
*Aayam Bansal,Keertan Balaji*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: OpenDiscoveryTrace是一个公开数据集，包含558条AI科学智能体轨迹，用于评估AI科学家的推理过程而非仅输出结果。每个轨迹记录9个字段（思考、工具调用、观察、错误、修订触发器、自我报告置信度等），涵盖124个科学任务（药物发现、材料科学、基因组学、文献分析）和7种模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基准测试仅评估最终输出（生成的代码、假设或论文），而丢弃了得出这些输出的推理过程。这使得无法审计科学方法论、诊断失败模式或区分系统性推理与幸运猜测。

Method: 构建包含558条完整AI科学智能体轨迹的数据集，每个步骤记录9个字段结构化信息。对363条LLM判断的轨迹进行试点分析，比较三种前沿模型（GPT-5.4、Claude Opus 4.6、Gemini 3.1 Pro）和四种开放权重模型的表现。

Result: 三种前沿模型成功率相当（84-89%），但Claude Opus 4.6的错误率是GPT-5.4的30倍（2.5 vs 0.08每轨迹，p<0.0001，Cliff's δ=0.613）。错误类型差异显著：Claude 66.7%为工具误用，GPT-5.4 83.6%为推理错误。

Conclusion: 过程追踪揭示了输出-only评估无法发现的行为差异。数据集、追踪模式、智能体框架和基准定义均以CC BY 4.0公开，支持过程级评估、科学智能体审计和AI治理研究。

Abstract: Existing benchmarks for autonomous AI scientists evaluate only final outputs---generated code, hypotheses, or papers---yet discard the reasoning process by which those outputs were obtained. This makes it impossible to audit scientific methodology, diagnose failure modes, or distinguish systematic reasoning from fortunate guessing. We present \textbf{{OpenDiscoveryTrace}}, a public dataset of 558 complete AI scientific agent trajectories that captures how models reason, not just what they produce. Each trajectory records a structured 9-field-per-step trace---including thoughts, tool calls, observations, errors, revision triggers, and self-reported confidence---as models execute 124 scientific tasks spanning drug discovery, materials science, genomics, and scientific literature analysis. The dataset covers seven models: three frontier models (GPT-5.4, Claude Opus 4.6, and Gemini 3.1 Pro; 124 trajectories each, fully balanced across domains and difficulty levels) and four open-weight models (Qwen2.5-7B, Mistral-7B-v0.3, Phi-3.5-mini, and Qwen2.5-1.5B; 30 each), plus 60 live-retrieval variant trajectories. Pilot analysis on 363 LLM-judged trajectories reveals that process traces expose behavioral differences invisible to output-only evaluation: all three frontier models achieve comparable success rates (84--89%), yet Claude Opus 4.6 produces 30$\times$ more errors than GPT-5.4 (2.5 vs. 0.08 per trajectory, $p < 0.0001$, Cliff's $δ= 0.613$), with qualitatively different error profiles---66.7% tool misuse for Claude versus 83.6% reasoning errors for GPT-5.4. We define five benchmark tasks with baselines from logistic regression, random forests, LSTMs, and Transformer models. The dataset, trace schema, agent harness, and benchmark definitions are publicly available under CC BY 4.0 to support research on process-level evaluation, scientific agent auditing, and AI governance.

</details>


### [86] [Adaptive Entangled Game Modules in Artificial General Intelligence](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.09226)
*Haochen Li,Xinshuai Guo,Jingdong Ouyang,Wei Zhang,Leilei Shi*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于概率波框架的广义行为智能(GBI)非局域概率波方程，用于建模交互适应主体的集体行为，并通过中国日内股票市场数据验证了LCA（刘-陈-敖）关于大脑非局域纠缠神经纤维的假说，发现82-94%的决策模式可由自适应纠缠博弈模式解释。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统新古典金融学基于独立理性主体假设难以解释市场中的复杂行为现象，同时LCA假说提出大脑中存在非局域纠缠神经纤维，但缺乏直接验证方法。研究旨在建立一种既能解释人类智能行为又能间接验证LCA假说的理论框架。

Method: 构建广义行为智能(GBI)非局域概率波方程，通过分析中国日内股票市场交易者行为数据，识别自适应纠缠博弈模式，并与独立理性模式进行对比分析。

Result: 自适应纠缠博弈模式解释了82-94%（总体89%）的观察到的决策模式；2-12%的行为表现出对日内新闻、事件和环境的适应，具有双重均衡状态和突变参考点特征；纯独立模式仅占不足5%。这些结果支持LCA假说。

Conclusion: 研究结果为LCA假说提供了实证支持，表明需要在人工通用智能(AGI)架构中整合自适应纠缠博弈模块，以克服传统人工神经网络AI的局限性，开发更紧凑、高效、稳健的类人处理单元(HPU)系统。

Abstract: We introduce a probability-wave framework for modeling the collective behavior of interacting adaptive agents, deriving testable eigenmodes through a generalized behavioral intelligence (GBI) nonlocal probability-wave equation. This framework captures a broad range of human intelligence behaviors with analytical mechanisms and offers an indirect method to examine the Liu-Chen-Ao (LCA) hypothesis of nonlocal entangled nerve fibers in the brain through collective trader behaviors. Our empirical analysis of Chinese intraday stock market data demonstrates that adaptive entangled game modes explain 82-94% (89% overall) of observed decision patterns, a sharp contrast to the predictions of neoclassical finance based on independent rational agents. Moreover, 2-12% of behaviors show adaption to intraday news, events, and environments, characterized by dual equilibrium states and abrupt reference point shifts, while purely independent modes occur in less than 5% of cases. These findings empirically support the LCA hypothesis, as observable trading behaviors reflect underlying brain mechanisms and internal intelligence decision-making in behavioral psychology. Our results highlight the necessity of incorporating adaptive entangled game modules into artificial general intelligence (AGI) architectures, addressing the limitations of conventional artificial neural network (ANN)-based AI, which relies on trillions of opaque parameters. By integrating ANN-based AI with probability-wave-based entangled-brain simulations, machine learning can enrich AGI foundation models (FMs) and facilitate the development of human-like processing units (HPUs) that leverage brain-inspired mechanisms. Such HPUs may ultimately create more compact, efficient, and robust AGI systems, particularly for embodied intelligence and robotics.

</details>


### [87] [Subagents vs Agent Skills: Executing Reusable Knowledge for Long-Horizon Agentic Tasks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.09233)
*Wasu Top Piriyakulkij,Rachel Lawrence,Alicia Curth,Sushrut Karmalkar,Niranjani Prasad*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 研究探讨了语言模型代理如何有效利用可重用知识库解决长周期任务，提出将技能包作为子代理调用而非加载到主上下文的方案，证明了该方案在技能包具有清晰输入输出契约时的优势。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解决长周期任务中传统方法的问题：随着上下文窗口信息增加，推理质量下降，导致基于加载技能指令的方法变得脆弱。

Method: 提出将技能包作为子代理调用的替代方案：子代理执行会生成专门用于解决子任务的新上下文窗口，而非将技能指令加载到主上下文中。

Result: 子代理执行优于代理-技能执行，前提是技能包具有清晰的输入输出契约且指令编码了满足这些契约所需的程序性知识。代价是额外的通信开销。

Conclusion: 可重用知识的效益不仅取决于其内容，还取决于其组织方式和调用方式。

Abstract: How can language model agents effectively leverage libraries of reusable knowledge to solve long-horizon tasks? Recent work has increasingly focused on agent skills: reusable capabilities represented as skill packages, i.e., multi-file bundles containing instructions, scripts, and other resources that help agents perform specific tasks. Agent skills are typically executed by loading their skill instructions into an agent's context and relying on the agent to follow them. As task horizons grow, however, this approach becomes increasingly brittle, because reasoning quality degrades as more information accumulates in the context window. We investigate an alternative approach in which skill packages are instead invoked as subagents. Rather than loading skill instructions into the main context, subagent execution spawns fresh context windows dedicated to solving individual subtasks. We show that subagent execution outperforms agent-skill execution when skill packages expose clear input-output contracts and their instructions encode the procedural knowledge needed to fulfill those contracts. The tradeoff is additional communication overhead, as extra tokens are required to coordinate between the main agent and its subagents. Our results show that the benefit of reusable knowledge depends not only on its content, but also on how it is organized and invoked.

</details>


### [88] [Gradland: On Phenomenal Experience, Differentiated Across Many Dimensions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.09306)
*David Balduzzi*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出一种假设：一阶结构（梯度/雅可比矩阵）可以表征现象体验的结构，并通过"Gradland"理想世界中的神经网络示例进行验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 旨在解释人类体验的各种特征，包括体验的持续时间、鲜明与模糊的差异、纹理体验、新生儿的混乱体验、观念的清晰与混淆、学习的感受，以及丰富密集体验的功能。

Method: 引入两种雅可比结构度量：有效秩(effective rank)和内聚性(cohesion)，基于Kirchhoff复杂度，并在一系列实例中应用验证。

Result: 该假设能够解释人类体验的多个方面：体验可延续数百毫秒；鲜明与模糊体验的差异；纹理体验；新生儿 buzzing confusion；观念的清晰与混淆；学习过程；以及丰富密集体验的功能。

Conclusion: 物理交互的一阶结构（梯度/雅可比矩阵）可能构成了现象体验结构的基础，这为理解意识体验提供了新的理论框架。

Abstract: This paper investigates the hypothesis that the first-order structure of physical interactions, i.e. gradients or Jacobians, characterizes the structure of phenomenal experience. It does so in an idealized world inhabited by neural networks, Gradland, where the physics are known and the functions are (mostly) differentiable. The paper introduces two measures of Jacobian structure: effective rank and cohesion, based on Kirchhoff complexity. Applying the measures to a series of worked examples shows the hypothesis accounts for: (1) the duration of experience, that it can prolong over hundreds of milliseconds; (2) the difference between what is experienced vividly and obscurely; (3) the experience of texture; (4) the blooming buzzing confusion presumably experienced by newborns; (5) the difference between ideas that are held distinctly in mind and ideas that are confused; (6) what learning is like; and finally (7) the paper explains the function of rich, dense experience.

</details>


### [89] [An Autonomous GeoAI Agent for Arctic Eco-Navigation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.09374)
*Samira Alkaee Taleghan,Younghyun Koo,Farnoush Banaei-Kashani*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于人类在环、多智能体的GeoAI系统，用于北极生态导航，将运营、物理、生态和社区相关标准整合到统一的路由框架中，解决现有方法忽略生态和社区影响的问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 北极航道规划是多标准问题，改善船舶安全或效率的路线可能增加对海冰、敏感生态系统和附近社区的暴露风险。现有方法优先考虑航行时间、燃料消耗和航行风险，往往忽视生态和社区影响。

Method: 引入多智能体协调系统，包含专门智能体负责：地理空间数据获取与准备、多目标路线生成、基于天空线的决策支持。生态标准明确考虑了对敏感区域的暴露，包括重要鱼类栖息地和海豹关键栖息地。

Result: 框架在考虑生态系统影响和潜在社区负担的同时，将重大价值判断置于人类控制之下，支持更安全、更透明、更符合社会责任的北极航行。

Conclusion: 该系统为北极航道规划提供了更全面的方法，平衡了运营效率、航行安全、生态保护和社区利益。公开提供了项目页面和代码。

Abstract: Arctic maritime navigation is becoming increasingly important as changing sea-ice conditions expand seasonal accessibility while simultaneously introducing substantial operational, environmental, and community risks. Arctic route planning is inherently a multi-criteria problem: routes that improve vessel safety or efficiency may increase exposure to sea ice, sensitive ecosystems, or nearby communities. Existing routing methods prioritize travel time, fuel use, and navigational risk, often overlooking ecological and community impacts. We introduce a human-in-the-loop, multi-agent GeoAI system for Arctic eco-navigation that integrates operational, physical, ecological, and community-related criteria within a unified routing framework. Multiple specialized agents coordinate geospatial data acquisition and preparation, multi-objective route generation, and skyline-based decision support. The ecological criteria explicitly account for exposure to sensitive areas, including Essential Fish Habitat and seal critical habitat. By considering these ecosystem impacts and potential community burdens while keeping consequential value judgments under human control, the framework supports safer, more transparent, and socially responsible Arctic navigation. Project page and code are publicly available. https://samiraat.github.io/Arctic-Eco-Navigation-Agent/, https://github.com/samiraat/Arctic-Eco-Navigation-Agent

</details>


### [90] [The Menu Is an Execution Prior: State-Path Tool Menus for Online Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.09395)
*Bo Yan,Weikai Lin,Song Wang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 针对工具代理在面对数千个接口的库时的问题，提出状态路径工具菜单(State-Path Tool Menu)框架，将菜单视为执行先验，通过编码器表示哪些工具可以从当前状态运行、输出如何满足后续输入、以及训练路径中哪些顺序会重复出现，从而在ToolBench上将在线成功率从0.737提升到0.898。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前代理面对包含数千个接口的工具库时，实际代理通过工具执行操作，而现有方法按请求相关性排序工具，可能暴露最终操作但遗漏或延迟不太明显的生产者工具，导致多步骤任务难以完成。

Method: 提出状态路径(State-Path)概念，即从可观察请求状态到期望结果的预执行路径；将工具菜单框架化为执行先验，包含编码器(表示哪些工具可从当前状态运行、输出如何满足后续输入、训练路径中哪些顺序重复出现)和检索器(覆盖可执行入口、缺失输入的生产者及最终操作)；使用重排序器将生产者置于消费者之前。

Result: 在ToolBench上，在线成功率从0.737提升至0.898，优于检索、重排序、生成和路由基线方法；状态路径菜单用32个工具覆盖比官方列表128个工具更完整的链条；成功增益在不同模型容量的执行器家族中持续存在。

Conclusion: 状态路径工具菜单框架能有效提升多步骤工具调用任务的完成率，通过学习从请求状态到期望结果的前置路径，使代理能够正确排序和选择所需工具。

Abstract: Language models act through tools, yet practical agents face libraries containing thousands of interfaces. We introduce the tool menu as the short, ordered subset of available tools shown to an agent before execution. The agent can call only tools in this menu. Multi-step tasks require the final action and the prerequisite tools that create its inputs in a usable order. Current constructors rank tools by request relevance, which can surface the final action while omitting or delaying less obvious producers. We introduce the state path, a pre-execution route from the observable request state to the desired outcome, and propose State-Path Tool Menu to learn it. Our framework treats the menu as an execution prior over these routes. Its encoder represents which tools can run from the current state, how their outputs satisfy later inputs, and which orders recur in training paths. A retriever covers an executable entry, the missing-input producers, and the final action. A reranker then places producers before consumers. On ToolBench, our menu raises online success from 0.737 to 0.898 and outperforms retrieval, reranking, generation, and routing baselines without changing the agent. The State-Path menu also covers more complete chains with 32 tools than the official list covers with 128, and its success gain persists across executor families with different model capacities. Our code is at https://github.com/Met2348/State-Path.

</details>


### [91] [Decision-Focused Active Learning for Scale-Aware Critical-Materials Recovery](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.09413)
*Niranjan Srinivas,Debajyoti Ray,Elias Nakouzi*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文研究稀有 earth 元素回收过程中的工艺选择优化问题，提出基于主动学习和贝叶斯风险的方法来减少实验次数并找到最佳回收工艺。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 选择回收工艺需要连接实验室结果与产品要求、工艺成本和规模效应。传统方法实验次数多、效率低，需要更智能的实验选择策略来优化稀有 earth 磁体回收过程。

Method: 采用主动学习方法（使用历史结果选择实验）、条件回顾性基准测试、贝叶斯方法（计算下游贝叶斯风险）、合成模拟以及两阶段重建策略。

Result: 主动学习方法在16-24口井（实验）内达到记录的最大富集度，而非自适应空间填充方法需要48口井；两阶段重建在16口井处达到最优；SmCo磁体Round 2存在纯度与产率的权衡；混合过滤策略的估计损失低于联合搜索。

Conclusion: 提出的基于预期下游贝叶斯风险最小的批选择方法具有优越性，需进行前瞻性测试验证，明确测量标准、工艺决策定义、经济输入和规模验证要求。

Abstract: Choosing a recovery process for scale-up requires connecting laboratory results with product requirements, process costs, and scale effects. We analyze records from Pacific Northwest National Laboratory's Computer Intelligence for Critical Element Recovery and Optimization (CICERO) workflow for autonomous selective precipitation. Active learning uses prior results to choose experiments. In a conditional retrospective benchmark with fitted models and recycled neodymium-iron-boron (NdFeB) magnet records, active learning finds the best recorded result with fewer experiments than nonadaptive space filling. Enrichment is the selected rare-earth-to-iron ratio relative to that in the feed. Adaptive policies reach the recorded enrichment maximum by 16 to 24 wells (individual experiments), versus 48. Our two-stage reconstruction ties two adaptive alternatives at 16 wells. Conditional analyses of recycled samarium-cobalt (SmCo) magnets show a Round 2 tradeoff between purity and nominal yield, the recovery fraction calculated from an assumed starting amount - NdFeB Round 1 routes differ in enrichment. Rankings for produced water from oil and gas extraction depend on phase and dilution assumptions requiring confirmation.
  We propose choosing batches by their expected reduction in downstream Bayes risk: the minimum expected loss among available process decisions under current beliefs. In exploratory simulations, a hybrid that filters candidates has lower estimated loss than the implemented joint search across routes and conditions. Differences involving the synthetic two-stage policy are small relative to estimation uncertainty. We outline a pre-registered prospective test under a shared loss and logging standard, requiring clarified measurements and records, a defined process decision and relevant outputs, credible economic inputs, and validation at the intended scale.

</details>


### [92] [Valerant: An Automatic Navigable Game Map Generator via Action-Conditioned World Model Exploration](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.09418)
*Yiran Qiao,Feng Wang,Jing Ma*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出Valerant框架，将预训练的动作条件世界模型转化为可用于探索和构建3D游戏地图的WAM（世界动作模型），通过结合视觉预测 rollout 与 SLAM 空间重建，实现从单张图像逐步生成可导航的3D游戏地图。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有游戏导向的方法主要在2D视觉观测空间操作，缺乏持久的3D几何表示。3D游戏需要持久可导航的空间和明确的几何结构来支持移动和交互，而动作条件视频预测只提供视觉观测，无法满足这一需求。如何将WAM范式扩展到3D游戏是一个重要挑战。

Method: 提出Valerant训练-free框架，包含三个核心组件：1）预训练动作条件世界模型用于视觉预测；2）SLAM-based空间重建用于构建持久3D几何；3）探索驱动动作选择引导agent逐步探索环境。从单张图像出发，通过迭代动作选择、视觉预测和空间建图，逐步构建完整3D游戏地图。

Result: 该框架成功将WAM交互从2D视觉模拟扩展到3D空间，为3D游戏地图创建提供新方法，可减少人工工作量。

Conclusion: Valerant展示了将预训练世界模型与空间重建结合构建3D游戏地图的可行性，为游戏内容自动生成提供了新思路，是WAM在游戏领域应用的重要进展。

Abstract: World Action Models (WAMs) couple predictive world modeling with action generation, allowing anticipated future states to guide agent behavior. Although WAMs are rapidly advancing embodied AI, general-purpose counterparts remain largely unexplored in games. Existing game-oriented approaches often combine action-conditioned world models with external policies and reward functions to realize WAM-like decision-making, yet they operate mainly in 2D visual observation space and do not instantiate persistent 3D geometry. Extending this paradigm to 3D games introduces a distinct challenge. In autonomous driving and robotics, the physical environment exists independently of the model, providing a persistent 3D world in which selected actions can be executed. Games have no such external substrate; the virtual world itself must be instantiated. Most playable games require a persistent and navigable space, while 3D games additionally require explicit geometry that supports movement and interaction. Action-conditioned video rollouts provide visual observations but not this spatial representation. We present \textsc{{Valerant}}, a training-free framework that transforms a pretrained action-conditioned world model into a WAM for exploring and constructing 3D game maps. By coupling predictive visual rollouts with SLAM-based spatial reconstruction and exploration-driven action selection, \textsc{{Valerant}} progressively transforms a single image into a persistent 3D game map. This framework extends WAM-based interaction beyond 2D visual simulation and offers a new approach to reducing manual effort in 3D game-map creation.

</details>


### [93] [XAI-Arena: Can LLMs Assess the Quality of XAI Explanations?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.09428)
*Yanfei Hu Fleischhauer,Alona Zharova,Nadja Klein,Stefan Feuerriegel*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 研究引入XAI-Arena框架，利用LLM作为评判者来评估可解释人工智能(XAI)解释的质量，解决传统方法依赖主观人类判断的局限性问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有XAI解释质量评估方法依赖主观人类判断，导致可重复性、可扩展性和研究间可比性受限，需要一种更标准化、可大规模应用的评估机制。

Method: 提出XAI-Arena框架，基于LLM-as-a-judge模式，从感知简洁性、清晰度、任务充分性、信任校准、可操作性、透明性、忠实度和整体可解释性等多维度评估XAI解释，并在多种数据集、机器学习模型和利益相关者角色上进行基准测试。

Result: 人类验证显示LLM生成评分与人类评分呈强正相关(Spearman's rho=.693, p<.001)，LLM评估能够捕捉XAI解释质量的系统性差异。

Conclusion: 基于LLM的评估可提供可扩展、可重复的XAI解释比较评估框架，为XAI方法的质量评估提供标准化解决方案。

Abstract: Evaluating the quality of explanations produced by explainable AI (XAI) methods remains challenging because existing approaches often rely on subjective human judgment, limiting reproducibility, scalability, and comparability between studies. We examine whether LLMs can serve as a reproducible and scalable mechanism to make comparative assessments of the quality of XAI explanations. We introduce XAI-Arena, an LLM-as-a-judge framework for scalable, reproducible, multidimensional, and stakeholder-sensitive evaluation of XAI explanation quality. XAI-Arena then allows us to compare XAI explanations along various dimensions, namely, perceived simplicity, clarity, task adequacy, trust calibration, actionability, transparency, faithfulness, and overall interpretability. We then benchmark XAI explanation methods across various datasets, machine learning models, and stakeholder personas. Human validation shows a strong positive association between LLM-generated and human ratings (Spearman's rho=.693, p<.001). Together, LLM-based evaluations can capture systematic differences in XAI explanation quality and provide a scalable and reproducible framework for comparative assessment of XAI explanations.

</details>


### [94] [Do Agents Know When They Succeed? Calibrating Agent Confidence from Internal Representations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.09448)
*Priyanka Mary Mammen,Emil Joswin,Srujananjali Medicherla*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 研究如何利用大语言模型的内部表示来预测智能体(agent)在多轮交互任务中的成功率，提出两种方法LTD和ARP来监测智能体的可靠性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 智能体系统正快速应用于安全关键场景，需要测量与智能体行为相关的置信度。与传统机器学习系统不同，智能体工作流具有复杂的失败模式，涉及规划、工具调用和动态环境交互，因此需要更可靠的可靠性监测方法。

Method: 提出两种互补方法：1) 潜在轨迹动力学(LTD)，总结交互轨迹中残差流表示的变化；2) 动作表示探针(ARP)，从动作决策时的表示预测成功与否。这些方法基于模型内部表示而非表面生成特征。

Result: 在三个交互基准测试(Bash、SQL、Python)和三个模型家族(Qwen14B、Qwen7B、DeepSeek6.7B)上，该方法始终优于表面层面生成和基于序列的校准基线方法。

Conclusion: 该方法是一种零开销的可靠性监测器，无需提示修改或多样本 rollout，为智能体系统的安全部署提供了有效的置信度评估手段。

Abstract: As agentic systems getting adopted rapidly in safety critical applications, it is vital to measure the confidence associated with the agentic actions. In comparison to the traditional machine learning systems, agentic workflows have complex failure modes with planning, tool invocation and dynamic environment interactions. In this paper, we investigate whether model's internal representations provide stronger signals of eventual task success in multi-turn agentic setups. We introduce two complementary methods: Latent Trajectory Dynamics (LTD), which summarizes changes in residual-stream representations across an an interaction trajectory, and the Action Representation Probe (ARP), which predicts success from representations formed at action decisions. Across three interactive benchmarks (Bash, SQL, Python) and three model families (Qwen14B, Qwen7B, DeepSeek6.7B), our methods consistently outperform surface level generation and sequence-based calibration baselines providing a zero-overhead reliability monitor that requires neither prompt alterations nor multi-sample rollouts.

</details>


### [95] [ContractEval: Query-Conditioned Execution Matching for Procedural Instruction Conformance](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.09458)
*Praphul Singh,Shanu Kumar,Akshat Agarwal,Ganesh Kumar*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: CONTRACTEVAL是一个诊断框架，用于显式识别LLM智能体在执行程序化任务时未完成的检查、分支、依赖或不变量等义务。它将程序指令表示为查询活跃义务，并与响应或跟踪证据匹配，将遗漏、错误分支、排序错误、额外操作、不变量违反和输出契约违反转化为不同的 conformance failures。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前LLM智能体从回答问题转向执行程序，失败可能是不可见的：最终响应看起来可以接受，但系统跳过了使答案合理所需的检查、分支、依赖或不变量。输出-only评估只能看到答案，trace-aware判断能看到活动，但都无法识别查询的活跃义务。

Method: 将程序指令表示为query-active obligations，并将其与响应或trace证据进行匹配。将遗漏、错误分支、排序错误、额外操作、不变量违反和输出-契约违反转化为不同的conformance failures。

Result: 在受控的审计程序契约套件上，output-only和trace-aware LLM judges错过了许多注入的结构性失败；在gold expected和observed graphs下，ContractEval能检测和定位所有失败。LLM-backed extraction保留了大量信号，但仍对校准敏感。

Conclusion: ContractEval不是合规保证；它使程序一致性可审计，而不是隐含在最终答案质量中。

Abstract: As LLM agents move from answering questions to carrying out procedures, failures can be unwarranted rather than visibly wrong: the final response looks acceptable even though the system skipped the check, branch, dependency, or invariant that made the answer justified. Output-only evaluation sees the answer, and trace-aware judging sees activity, but neither identifies which obligations were active for the query. We introduce CONTRACTEVAL, a diagnostic framework for making those active obligations explicit. It represents procedural instructions as query-active obligations and matches them against response or trace evidence, turning omissions, wrong branches, ordering errors, extra actions, invariant breaches, and output-contract violations into distinct conformance failures. On a controlled suite of audited procedural contracts, output-only and trace-aware LLM judges miss many injected structural failures; under gold expected and observed graphs, ContractEval detects and localizes all of them. LLM-backed extraction preserves much of this signal but remains calibration-sensitive. ContractEval is therefore not a compliance guarantee; it makes procedural conformance auditable rather than implicit in final-answer quality.

</details>


### [96] [Multi-Agent Agentic Graph Learning via Structural Signatures](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.09565)
*Liang Qu,Jianxin Li,Hua Wang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了MAAGL框架，这是一种多智能体代理图学习方法。该框架将图划分为社区，为每个社区分配独立的智能体进行区域特定的专业化推理。通过将结构证据表示为动态更新的排列不变且大小固定的结构签名，将语义证据过滤到相关性排名靠前的top-k节点，并根据具有相似签名的历史轨迹估计智能体的置信度，在需要时触发辩论式协作。实验表明，MAAGL在四个基准数据集上优于现有的最先进的代理图学习方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的代理图学习(AGL)方法要么使用单一智能体，要么编排多个基于角色的智能体进行推理和学习，但本质上都在不同图区域依赖共享的推理策略，这对于具有异构图结构和语义模式的图来说是次优的。受到多智能体协作在复杂推理任务上进展的启发，一个自然的解决方案是让多个智能体拥有不同的记忆并进行协作。然而，直接将这种范式应用于图面临两个挑战：首先，现有的AGL方法通常将图结构转化为自然语言描述，使推理过程对结构信息的排序敏感，从而破坏了图的排列不变性质；其次，融入越来越大的采样邻域会导致上下文快速增长。

Method: MAAGL框架采用以下方法：(1)将图划分为社区，为每个社区分配独立的智能体以实现区域特定的专业化；(2)将结构证据和语义证据分开表示，结构证据由动态更新的结构签名总结，该签名具有排列不变性且大小固定，而语义证据被过滤到按相关性排名的top-k节点；(3)基于具有相似签名的历史轨迹，智能体估计其置信度并在需要时触发辩论式协作。

Result: 在四个基准数据集上的广泛实验表明，MAAGL优于现有的最先进的代理图学习方法。

Conclusion: MAAGL通过多智能体协作、区域特定专业化、排列不变的结构表示和选择性协作机制，有效解决了异构图上的推理问题，为代理图学习领域提供了一种新的研究方向。

Abstract: Agentic graph learning (AGL) has recently achieved promising results on graph reasoning tasks, where an agent powered by a large language model (LLM) sequentially samples the graph as evidence to support its final prediction. Existing methods either employ a single agent or orchestrate multiple role-based agents to reason and learn over the entire graph, but both essentially rely on a shared reasoning policy across different graph regions, which can be suboptimal for graphs with heterogeneous structural and semantic patterns. Inspired by the progress of multi-agent collaboration on complex reasoning tasks, a natural remedy is to let multiple agents own different memory and collaborate; however, applying this paradigm to graphs directly faces two challenges. First, existing AGL methods typically verbalize graph structures into natural-language descriptions for LLM agents, making the reasoning process sensitive to the ordering of structural information and thereby breaking the permutation-invariant nature of graphs. Second, incorporating increasingly large sampled neighborhoods leads to rapidly growing contexts. To address these challenges, this paper introduces a multi-agent agentic graph learning (i.e., MAAGL) framework. MAAGL partitions the graph into communities and assigns an independent agent to each community for region-specific specialization. MAAGL represents structural and semantic evidence separately. Structural evidence is summarized by a dynamically updated structural signature that is permutation-invariant and fixed in size, while semantic evidence is filtered to the top-k nodes ranked by relevance. Based on historical trajectories with similar signatures, agents estimate their confidence and trigger debate-style collaboration when needed. Extensive experiments on four benchmark datasets show that MAAGL outperforms SOTA AGL methods.

</details>


### [97] [CityPlanner: A Sandbox Agent for Executable Urban Planning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.09578)
*Wentao Zhang,Jingyuan Wang,Zetong Zhou,Yifan Yang,Wenrui Wang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: CityPlanner是一个用于可执行城市规划的沙盒智能体框架，通过UrbanSandbox统一文件化环境和原子任务强化学习方法，将长轨迹分解为BuildPlan和ImprovePlan两个阶段，使城市规划学习变得可处理，并在真实世界基准上取得最优性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 城市规划是一个现实世界的空间优化问题，需要在成本和服务质量等实际目标下从大型候选空间中选择可行动作。现有优化和强化学习方法对固定公式有效，但通常依赖于任务特定表示和约束处理，缺乏通用性和灵活性。

Method: 提出CityPlanner框架，包含UrbanSandbox统一文件化环境和原子任务强化学习。UrbanSandbox让智能体检查任务文件、生成计划、运行评估器并根据可执行反馈修订决策。原子任务RL将长沙盒轨迹分解为BuildPlan（初始构建）和ImprovePlan（基于反馈的改进）两个阶段，使学习变得可处理。

Result: 在真实世界基准上的实验表明，CityPlanner consistently outperforms启发式方法、任务特定RL和通用LLM-agent基线。消融实验验证了UrbanSandbox、原子任务RL和迭代部署的贡献。

Conclusion: CityPlanner通过城市沙盒环境和原子任务强化学习方法，为可执行的城市规划提供了新的范式。UrbanSandbox提供了统一的文件化环境，使智能体能够检查任务文件、生成计划、运行评估器并根据可执行反馈修订决策。原子任务RL将长轨迹分解为BuildPlan和ImprovePlan两个阶段，使学习变得可处理。实验表明该方法在真实世界基准上 consistently outperforms baselines。

Abstract: Urban planning is a real-world spatial optimization problem that requires selecting feasible actions from large candidate spaces under practical objectives such as cost and service quality. Existing optimization and reinforcement learning methods are effective for fixed formulations, but often depend on task-specific representations and constraint handling. We propose \emph{{CityPlanner}}, a sandbox-agent framework for executable urban planning. CityPlanner introduces \emph{{UrbanSandbox}}, a unified file-based environment where agents inspect task files, generate plans, run evaluators, and revise decisions based on executable feedback. To make learning tractable, we further propose atomic-task reinforcement learning, which decomposes long sandbox trajectories into \emph{{BuildPlan}} for initial construction and \emph{{ImprovePlan}} for feedback-based refinement. Experiments on a real-world benchmark show that CityPlanner consistently outperforms heuristic, task-specific RL, and general LLM-agent baselines. Ablations verify the contributions of UrbanSandbox, atomic-task RL, and iterative deployment. We release the code and dataset at https://anonymous.4open.science/r/co-agent-C1C8

</details>


### [98] [A Function-Space Approach to the Statistical Mechanics of Learning Dynamics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.09589)
*Yizhou Zhang,Weichen Wu,Lun Du,Zhengjie Miao*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文从统计力学角度研究深度神经网络在函数空间中的学习动力学，将参数配置视为微观态，函数及其动力学算子视为宏观变量。针对均方误差损失，推导出学习算子M=JJ*的精确误差动力学，并结合统计算子B提出了一个新的波动势函数。该理论预测当[M,B]=0时系统旋转平稳，通过配对大特征值与小特征值实现能量极小化，对ReLU网络揭示了低结构曲率对应更快松弛的特性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 深度神经网络在参数空间极其复杂非线性的情况下，却展现出规律性的宏观行为。这一现象缺乏理论解释。作者试图建立一套直接在函数空间中描述学习的统计力学框架，将参数配置作为微观实现，将函数作为宏观变量，从而理解网络学习的稳定集体组织机制。

Method: 1. 将参数配置作为微观态，函数及其动力学算子作为宏观变量
2. 对于均方损失，推导学习算子M=JJ*的精确误差动力学
3. 结合条件随机动力学和参数空间密度态，定义统计算子B（由局部曲率确定）
4. 对局部波动进行积分，得到波动势函数Φ_fluc(M;B)
5. 对ReLU型函数空间，在 mild stable 统计条件下分析B的结构

Result: 1. 推导得到波动势函数：Φ_fluc(M;B) = (σ_ξ²/2)log det(M⁻¹ + B) + const
2. 在固定谱条件下，当[M,B]=0时系统旋转平稳
3. 势函数极小化要求将M的大特征值与B的小特征值配对
4. 产生对抗旋转失配的局部恢复力
5. 对于ReLU网络：B = σ_ξ²L*KL（L测量粗粒化二阶结构）
6. 低B区域对应低结构曲率，导致沿平滑、数据自适应方向的更快松弛

Conclusion: 函数空间是研究学习中稳定集体组织的自然宏观层次。该理论框架统一了参数空间和函数空间的视角，为理解深度学习泛化、收敛性和架构设计提供了新的理论工具。核心洞察是学习动力学偏好沿低曲率的光滑数据自适应方向演化。

Abstract: Deep neural networks exhibit regular macroscopic behavior despite highly nonlinear dynamics in vast parameter spaces. We develop a statistical-mechanical description of learning directly in function space, treating parameter configurations as microscopic realizations and functions with their dynamical operators as macroscopic variables. For mean-squared loss, the exact error dynamics are governed by the learning operator \(M=JJ^\ast\). Combining the dynamical Boltzmann weight of the conditional stochastic dynamics with the parameter-space density of states, whose local curvature defines a statistical operator \(B\), and integrating over local fluctuations yields
  $$ Φ_{{\mathrm{{fluc}}}}(M;B)=\frac{{σ_ξ^2}}{{2}}\log\det(M^{{-1}}+B)+\mathrm{{const}}. $$
  At fixed spectrum, this term is rotationally stationary when \([M,B]=0\), is minimized by pairing large eigenvalues of \(M\) with small eigenvalues of \(B\), and generates a local restoring contribution against rotational mismatch. For ReLU-type function spaces under mild stable statistical conditions, \(B=σ_ξ^2L^\ast\mathcal K L\), where \(L\) measures coarse-grained second-order structure. Thus the low-\(B\) sector corresponds, up to bounded anisotropy of \(\mathcal K\), to low structural curvature, implying a preference for faster relaxation along smooth, data-adaptive directions. These results identify function space as a natural macroscopic level for studying stable collective organization in learning.

</details>


### [99] [From State Synchronization to Cognitive Self-Evolution: An Operational Architecture for Cognitive Digital Twins](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.09625)
*Haoran Gao,An Li,Zhen Li,Jun Cai*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了一个四层认知数字孪生(CDT)架构，包含物理层、数字孪生层、认知层和任务层，建立了自进化的闭环运营机制。架构通过知识、记忆和注意力构建任务特定的认知模型，支持用户请求驱动和自驱动两种认知模式。仿真研究表明在有限语义信息下可实现可靠的闭环任务执行，并通过累积任务经验提高运营效率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有CDT研究主要关注特定使能技术(如学习模块、知识图谱、大语言模型)，但缺乏对认知能力如何系统性地整合到DT架构中的深入理解。文章旨在解决这一系统性整合问题。

Method: 提出四层 CDT 架构(物理层、数字孪生层、认知层、任务层)，建立跨这四层的自进化闭环运营循环。物理状态同步到数字表示，认知通过知识、记忆和注意力构建任务特定的认知模型，任务级决策在实际约束下生成。运营反馈进一步优化认知体验，更新数字表示中的关系和注解。采用轻量级仿真验证。

Result: 在有限语义信息下实现可靠的闭环任务可行性，通过累积任务经验提高运营效率。讨论了语义通信、知识查询、任务编排和闭环同步等关键使能机制及部署挑战。

Conclusion: 所提框架为未来CDT系统的设计和开发提供了结构化基础，建立了一个系统的认知整合架构，为DT从状态同步向任务导向和知识驱动运营演进提供了理论支撑。

Abstract: As Digital Twin (DT) systems evolve beyond state synchronization toward task-oriented and knowledge-driven operation, Cognitive Digital Twins (CDTs) have emerged as an extension that incorporates cognitive capabilities into twin operation. Existing CDT studies often focus on specific enabling techniques, such as learning modules, knowledge graphs, and large language models, while providing limited insight into how cognition can be systematically integrated into DT architectures. To address this issue, this paper proposes a four-layer CDT architecture consisting of the physical layer, digital-twin layer, cognitive layer, and task layer. The proposed architecture establishes a self-evolving closed operational loop spanning these four layers, in which physical states are synchronized into digital representations, cognition constructs task-specific cognitive models through knowledge, memory, and attention, and task-level decisions are generated under practical constraints. Operational feedback further refines cognitive experience and updates relationships and annotations in the digital representation, enabling subsequent task interpretation, initiation, and reasoning to evolve with system operation. Based on this framework, two representative operation modes are characterized: user-request-driven cognition and self-driven cognition. We further discuss key enabling mechanisms and deployment challenges associated with semantic communication, knowledge querying, task orchestration, and closed-loop synchronization. A lightweight simulation study illustrates reliable closed-loop task feasibility under limited semantic information and improved operational efficiency through accumulated task experience. The proposed framework provides a structured foundation for the design and development of future CDT systems.

</details>


### [100] [Seven Sources of Physical AI Capability Formation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.09627)
*Gang Chen*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了一个基于"能力形成来源"(capability-formation source)的新分类框架，用于理解Physical AI系统中各种能力是如何产生的。该框架识别了七种非排他性的能力来源：记录经验(RE)、预测建模(PM)、评估交互(EI)、替代环境(SE)、机制接地(MG)、具身耦合(EC)和进化驱动(ED)形成。通过重建归纳法和理论饱和度分析，对49条证据记录进行编码验证，证实所有挑战均可由这七种来源单独或组合解释，未发现第八种不可约的来源。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有Physical AI的分类方法（按形态、架构、学习算法、任务或领域）无法直接回答"能力是如何形成的"这一核心问题。不同形态的Physical AI可能具有相似的表面能力，但其形成机制可能截然不同。需要一个新的分析框架来区分"能力相似性"和"形成方式相似性"。

Method: 采用重建归纳法(reconstructive induction)配合理论饱和度(theoretical saturation)分析。具体步骤包括：1)追溯研究矩阵至原始研究；2)文献去重；3)设定编码规则；4)进行三轮最大差异采样和负案例采样。编码过程中识别出与多个挑战相关的来源，包括课程学习和自监督学习、主动推理、开放域和发展学习、规划和搜索、神经符号架构、数字孪生、生成式物理世界模型以及形态-控制协同设计等。

Result: 在2026年9月4日确定的范围和标准内，全部49条证据记录均可由七种来源单独或组合解释。R1-R3挑战均未产生不可约的第八种来源，R3无需新的核心定义或实质性边界规则。研究声称在所述范围内达到理论饱和，但不代表逻辑完整性或对未来覆盖的穷尽性。

Conclusion: 该框架能够在给定范围内达到理论饱和，可有效区分"能力表现相似性"与"能力形成方式相似性"。该框架支持对解释性、迁移性、复制性、依赖性、治理证据和地缘经济基础等方面的分析。同时研究承认其局限性：理论饱和不等于逻辑完备，未来可能出现新的挑战需要扩展；研究范围受限于2026年9月的文献，时间边界外可能存在未覆盖的来源。

Abstract: Capabilities relevant to Physical AI can arise from materially different formation histories, yet existing taxonomies organized by morphology, architecture, learning algorithm, task, or domain do not directly answer what gives rise to a capability. We define a capability-formation source as a factor materially contributing to capability formation, distinct from components or construction steps. We identify seven non-exclusive sources: Recorded-Experience (RE), Predictive-Modeling (PM), Evaluative-Interaction (EI), Surrogate-Environment (SE), Mechanism-Grounded (MG), Embodied-Coupling (EC), and Evolution-Driven (ED) Formation. Using reconstructive induction with theoretical saturation, we traced a research matrix to primary studies, deduplicated the literature, set coding rules, and conducted three rounds of maximum-difference and negative-case sampling. Challenges included curriculum and self-supervised learning, active inference, open-ended and developmental learning, planning and search, neuro-symbolic architectures, digital twins, generative physical world models, and morphology-control co-design. Within the scope and criteria fixed as of September 4, 2026, all 49 evidence records were explainable by the seven sources individually or in combination. No R1-R3 challenge produced an irreducible eighth source, and R3 required no new core definition or substantive boundary rule. We therefore claim theoretical saturation within the stated scope, not logical completeness or exhaustive future coverage. The framework distinguishes similarity in observed capability from similarity in how it was formed, supporting analysis of explanation, transfer, replication, dependencies, governance evidence, and geoeconomic foundations.

</details>


### [101] [RobustSGPO: Search-Space Control for Agent Harness Evolution](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.09646)
*Zibo Zhao,Jijun Shi,Mo Zhou,Zhongyuan Wang,Shifu Bie,Yunfei Zhang,Xuanting Zhou,Xiangyu Wu,Bin Liu,Ruiming Tang,Wenwu Ou,Kun Gai*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: A method called RobustSGPO improves semantic-gradient-based prompt optimization by specifying edit scope, constructing and checking patches, and continuing search from incumbent or retained snapshots.


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: SGPO improves agent harnesses using execution feedback but its local update rule does not resolve the choice of edit scope and operation.

Method: RobustSGPO introduces a mechanism to specify requested edits, construct and verify patches, and continue search from either incumbent or retained snapshots. It evaluates permission scheduling, cumulative controls, and task-family transfer in AgentX brainstorming workflow.

Result: Periodic 1→2→3 scheduling outperforms fixed maximum permission by 0.28 test-score points. RobustSGPO increases completion on 30 held-out tasks from 60% to 80% and improves test quality from 3.77 to 4.14 under a 20-million-token budget. Category retention reduces source-task degradation after shift, while random retention reaches higher destination endpoint.

Conclusion: Search-space control via executable edits and alternative starting points improves quality, albeit with measurable retention overhead.

Abstract: Semantic-gradient-based prompt optimization (SGPO) improves agent harnesses using execution feedback, but its local update rule leaves the choice of edit scope and operation unresolved. We introduce RobustSGPO, which specifies the requested edit, constructs and checks the patch, and continues search from either the incumbent or retained snapshots. We evaluate permission scheduling, cumulative controls, and task-family transfer in the AgentX brainstorming workflow using 120 tasks, 95 runs, and 7,350 candidate attempts. Periodic $1\to2\to3$ scheduling exceeds fixed maximum permission by 0.28 test-score points. RobustSGPO increases completion on 30 held-out tasks from 60.0% to 80.0% and improves test quality from 3.77 to 4.14 under a 20-million-token budget. Category retention reduces source-task degradation after a shift, whereas random retention reaches a higher destination endpoint. Search-space control benefits quality through executable edits and alternative starting points, with measurable retention overhead.

</details>


### [102] [Black-Box Red Teaming of Agentic AI: A Taxonomy-Driven Framework for Automated Risk Discovery](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.09647)
*Divyanshu Kumar,Nitin Aravind Birur,Tanay Baswa,Sahil Agarwal,Prashanth Harshangi*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种针对Agentic系统的系统性黑盒风险评估框架，通过七域分类法、自动化红队测试和LLM评判者来评估多步骤代理漏洞，实现在无需特权访问的情况下识别关键架构安全问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Agentic系统正在进入生产环境，可读取不可信输入、调用具有真实权限的工具并自主行动，但现有标准评估仍是单轮的，无法捕捉多步骤代理的复杂漏洞，因此需要系统性的风险感知评估方法。

Method: 提出三部分框架：(1)七域分类法将可观察行为映射到风险类别；(2)完全自动化的SAGE-RT红队测试，每个域产生120个对抗场景；(3)使用LLM评判者进行人工验证评估。在CrewAI和AutoGen两种架构、四个基础模型上验证。

Result: 56.25%平均治理风险，多代理配置中65%隐私风险，代理行为漏洞达85%，揭示了多步骤代理系统中的严重安全隐患。

Conclusion: 黑盒方法可有效识别关键架构漏洞，无需特权访问，为更安全的Agentic系统部署提供了可扩展的风险评估路径。

Abstract: Agentic systems are rapidly moving to production, where they read untrusted inputs, call tools with real permissions, and act autonomously, expanding the security surface beyond chat-only models. Yet standard evaluations remain single-turn and fail to capture multi-step agent vulnerabilities. We present a systematic black-box framework for risk-aware agent evaluation requiring only basic system descriptions. Our approach introduces: (1) a seven-domain taxonomy mapping observable behaviors to risk categories, (2) fully automated SAGE-RT red teaming producing 120 adversarial scenarios per domain, and (3) human-validated evaluation using LLM judges. Empirical validation across two agent architectures (CrewAI and AutoGen) with four base models reveals alarming patterns: 56.25\% average governance risk, 65\% privacy risk in multi-agent configurations, and agent behavior vulnerabilities reaching 85\%. Our black-box approach effectively identifies critical architectural vulnerabilities without privileged access, providing a scalable path toward safer agent deployments.

</details>


### [103] [RESCUE-BENCH: Towards Relation-Aware Multi-Party Emotional Support Conversation Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.09657)
*Haichuan Hu,Yang Xiao,Mingni Tang,Jiawen Duan,Quanjun Zhang,Congqing He,Hao Zhang,Jiashuo Wang,Johan F. Hoorn,Wenjie Li*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出关系感知情绪支持对话任务，构建了RESCUE基准数据集，包含191个样本、7079个标注轮次和1064.8分钟视频。实验表明当前LLM在依赖局部情绪线索的任务上表现较好，但在关系密集型任务上存在明显不足。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有情绪支持对话系统主要关注一对一场景，忽略多方情境下的人际关系动态。文章旨在探索LLM能否利用关系动态提供更有效的情绪支持。

Method: 构建RESCUE数据集，来源于真实夫妻和家庭访谈对话。基于丰富的社会情感和支持动态标注，定义了六个任务评估关系感知情绪支持的两种核心能力：关系理解和关系敏感支持。对十个LLM进行实验评估。

Result: 当前模型在依赖局部情绪或干预线索的任务上表现相对较好，但在关系模式预测、观点预测和支持策略预测等关系密集型任务上表现困难。

Conclusion: 揭示了当前LLM在人际关系建模和关系敏感支持决策方面的局限性，为未来关系感知情绪支持对话研究指明方向。

Abstract: Existing emotional support conversation systems mainly focus on one-on-one seeker-supporter interactions and individual emotional states, leaving interpersonal relations in multi-party scenarios underexplored. In this work, we introduce relation-aware emotional support conversation, a new task that evaluates whether LLMs can capture and utilize the evolving dynamics of relationships to offer more effective emotional support. We construct RESCUE (Relation-aware Emotional Support Conversation Understanding and Evaluation Benchmark) from real couple and family interview conversations, containing 191 samples, 7,079 annotated turns, and 1,064.8 minutes of video. Based on rich annotations of socio-emotional and support-related dynamics, RESCUE defines six tasks that evaluate two core capabilities required for relation-aware emotional support: Relational Understanding and Relation-Sensitive Support. Experiments with ten LLMs show that current models perform relatively well on tasks relying on local emotional or intervention cues, but struggle with relation-intensive tasks such as relation pattern prediction, viewpoint prediction, and support strategy prediction. These findings reveal the limitations of current LLMs in modeling interpersonal relations and making relation-sensitive support decisions.

</details>


### [104] [PRAGMA: Evaluating Personalized Guidance with Memory Alignment in Lifelong Conversations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.09664)
*Hyojeong Yu,Hyukhun Koh,Minsung Kim,Yunah Jang,Kyomin Jung*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: PRAGMA是一个评估长对话中个性化指导的基准测试，旨在填补现有对话记忆评估主要关注检索和事实回忆的空白。基准包含纵向对话历史、证据注释和基于不断变化用户上下文和错误用户假设的指导场景。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着对话变长，依靠完整交互历史变得效率低下且不可靠；现有对话记忆评估主要关注检索和事实回忆，缺乏对个性化指导（如推荐、规划、决策支持）的研究。

Method: 构建PRAGMA基准，包含纵向对话历史、证据注释和指导场景；对检索系统、记忆系统和长上下文模型进行实验评估。

Result: 当前系统在恢复适当的对话证据和有效利用证据进行个性化指导方面都存在困难。

Conclusion: 需要支持稳健对话检索和超越证据回忆的记忆架构，以实现记忆 grounding 的推理。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) are increasingly deployed as personalized assistants that interact with users over extended periods of time. As conversations grow longer, relying on full interaction histories becomes increasingly inefficient and unreliable: long contexts introduce substantial computational overhead, making it difficult for models to consistently identify and utilize the most relevant information for the current request. These challenges have motivated memory systems that structure and retrieve user-specific information. In realistic interactions, users often seek practical guidance such as recommendations, planning, and decision support. Unlike factual recall tasks, personalized guidance requires models to integrate information across multiple past conversations and reason about changing user preferences and experiences. However, existing conversational memory evaluations mainly focus on retrieval and factual recall. To study this challenge, we introduce PRAGMA, a benchmark for evaluating personalized guidance in long-term conversations. PRGAMA contains curated longitudinal conversation histories, evidence annotations, and guidance scenarios grounded in evolving user contexts and incorrect user assumptions. Experiments across retrieval systems, memory systems, and long-context models reveal that current systems struggle both to recover the appropriate conversational evidence and to effectively use it for personalized guidance. Our results highlight the need for memory architectures that support robust conversational retrieval and memory-grounded reasoning beyond evidence recall.

</details>


### [105] [Safe to Stop? Risk-Constrained Stopping for Sequential Clinical Diagnosis Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.09678)
*Yuexin Wu,Vasile Rus*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出Cros，一种风险约束的停止决策层，用于临床诊断智能体的自主停止决策。该方法结合状态级错误排序、不相交开发集的策略设计，以及选择性诊断错误和最小自主覆盖的精确检验。在MIMIC衍生的腹痛基准数据集上，该方法相比基线方法显著提升了错误排序能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有智能体基准测试主要评估固定或无约束交互后的准确性，而将自主停止可靠性作为隐含指标。临床诊断智能体不仅需要决定下一步请求什么测试，还需要决定何时诊断或推迟，因此需要显式评估停止决策的可靠性。

Method: Cros方法包含三个核心组件：1) 状态级错误排序 2) 在不相交开发集上设计策略 3) LTT风格的选择性诊断错误和最小自主覆盖的精确检验。其有限样本保证要求候选族、测试规则和任何随机化在访问校准标签前冻结。

Result: 在1834个回合的MIMIC衍生腹痛基准上，完整排序器达到0.853的探索性状态-错误AUROC，优于最大类概率的0.715和骨干网络原生停止分数的0.552。在367个回合的评估集上，使用冻结的Cros权重平均得到16.9%的选择性错误率、78.8%覆盖度，成本5.57，平均0.68次测试；相比之下原生停止为30.8%错误率、100%覆盖度，成本8.14，平均1.53次测试。强制继续呈非单调性：单独使用HPI时错误率为28.3%，完整检查后为34.3%。

Conclusion: 由于评估标签在早期开发中被检查过，这些发现提供了探索性可行性和审计证据，而非确认性安全证书。Cros在20个开发重分割中仅在6个中满足联合标准，说明该方法的稳健性有限，需要进一步验证。

Abstract: Clinical diagnosis agents must decide not only what test to request next, but also when to diagnose or defer. Existing agent benchmarks largely evaluate accuracy after fixed or unconstrained interaction, leaving autonomous stopping reliability implicit. We present Cros, a risk-constrained stopping layer combining state-wise error ranking, policy design on disjoint development splits, and LTT-style exact tests of selective diagnostic error and minimum autonomous coverage for complete sequential policies. Its finite-sample guarantee requires the candidate family, testing rule, and any randomization to be frozen before calibration labels are accessed. On a 1,834-episode MIMIC-derived abdominal-pain benchmark, the full ranker achieves exploratory state-error AUROC 0.853, compared with 0.715 for maximum class probability and 0.552 for the backbone's native stop score. On the previously viewed 367-episode evaluation split, analytically averaging over the frozen Cros weights yields 16.9% selective error at 78.8% coverage, cost 5.57, and 0.68 tests, versus 30.8% error at 100% coverage, cost 8.14, and 1.53 tests under native stopping. Forced continuation is non-monotone: error is 28.3% with HPI alone and 34.3% after full workup. However, the uniform-weight mixture ablation is cheaper on this viewed split despite missing the locked development margins, and Cros nominally satisfies the joint criterion in only 6 of 20 development resplits. Because evaluation labels were inspected during earlier development, these findings provide exploratory feasibility and audit evidence, not a confirmatory safety certificate.

</details>


### [106] [Decision Shifts, Lost Label Functionality, and an Inconclusive Grounding Audit in Correctness-Gated Multi-Teacher Distillation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.09702)
*Xiaofei Feng*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 论文研究正确性门控多教师蒸馏方法，通过8个实验组比较正确性加权蒸馏与硬过滤等方法对学生模型的影响，发现在准确率、macro-F1等指标上有一定提升，但未能在决策层面建立优势。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究正确性门控多教师蒸馏是否能提升学生模型的决策正确性和推理依据 grounding，与硬过滤基线进行比较，探讨不同蒸馏策略的效果。

Method: 8个实验组共享4,330个源、63.9M参数学生模型、12,990个优化步骤、406次更新，使用证据输入和decoder；7个基于教师的组使用固定的三响应池。在267个保留样本上评估3个随机种子，并进行可用性修正审计。

Result: 正确性加权组相比无过滤蒸馏：准确率+0.1660、五标签macro-F1 +0.1323、条件不安全率-0.4979。但源标签SFT有最高mean macro-F1 (0.586)，加权组在所有种子中Refuted召回率为零，两个种子将所有167个claim样本分配为NotEnoughInfo。审计无法估计共同源 grounding 效应。

Conclusion: 硬过滤已实现0.660准确率、0.530 macro-F1和0.135条件不安全率。加权组未展示超越硬过滤的决策增益。该固定矩阵失败分析显示决策重新分配与标签功能丧失，可用的人类审计无法建立grounding增益。

Abstract: Candidate decision correctness and rationale grounding are different objectives. We examine correctness-gated multi-teacher distillation in a fixed experiment. Eight arms share 4,330 sources, a 63.9M-parameter student, 12,990 optimization rows, 406 updates, evidence inputs, and a decoder; seven teacher-based arms use one fixed three-response pool. Three seeds are evaluated on 267 held-out examples. Relative to unfiltered distillation, the correctness-weighted arm differed in accuracy by +0.1660 (95% observed-matrix interval [0.0670, 0.2455]), five-label macro-F1 by +0.1323 ([0.0916, 0.1731]), and task-defined conditional unsafe-action rate by -0.4979 ([-0.5926, -0.3686]). These shifts do not imply uniformly better behavior. Source-label SFT had the highest mean macro-F1 (0.586). The weighted arm had zero Refuted recall in every seed, and two seeds assigned NotEnoughInfo to all 167 claim examples. In an availability-amended audit at one reference seed, weighted and unfiltered outputs had 0/20 versus 1/20 evidence-supported positives and 20/20 versus 19/20 positives containing unsupported material. Samples were non-paired, source overlap was not serialized, and the amendment followed automatic summarization but preceded annotation. The audit therefore cannot estimate a common-source grounding effect and is inconclusive about system-level improvement or harm. Hard filtering already achieved 0.660 accuracy, 0.530 macro-F1, and 0.135 conditional unsafe rate. The implemented weighted arm showed no demonstrated incremental decision benefit over hard filtering. This fixed-matrix failure analysis shows decision redistribution with lost label functionality; the available human audit does not establish a grounding gain.

</details>


### [107] [Which Tokens Should SFT Actually Learn? A Token-Trimming Perspective on Mathematical Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.09707)
*Yaning Jia,Chunhui Zhang,Wenxuan Xu,Xingjian Diao,Xiaoyuan Wang,Soroush Vosoughi*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出TrimSFT方法，通过根据黄金token与其最强竞争对手之间的logit差距来缩放SFT损失，对数学推理的token级进行重新加权。该方法从两端（已掌握token和低置信度token）削减监督，在中间logit-gap区域集中学习。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前SFT对所有目标token应用统一的交叉熵损失，但不同token提供不均匀的学习信号。这种统一处理会过度强化已掌握token，同时增加不确定token的学习压力，导致训练效果次优。

Method: TrimSFT是一种简单的token级重加权方法，根据黄金token与其最强竞争对手之间的logit差距来缩放SFT损失。使用以margin m为中心、带宽为τ的高斯权重，不需要参考模型或额外前向传播。带宽τ比确切margin位置更重要。

Result: 在来自Llama、Qwen和DeepMath家族的六个基础模型上评估TrimSFT，在五个数学推理基准上，TrimSFT在六个模型中的五个上获得最佳平均性能，在MATH500上比SFT提升高达+26.9分。token级logit-gap分布分析表明，TrimSFT比统一SFT或单调重加权方法更平衡地重塑模型置信度。

Conclusion: 推理SFT可以从双端trimming中受益，而不是将所有token统一对待。TrimSFT通过在中间logit-gap区域集中学习，实现了更优的训练动态和性能提升。

Abstract: Supervised fine-tuning (SFT) applies a uniform cross-entropy loss to all target tokens, even though different tokens provide unequal learning signals for mathematical reasoning. This uniform treatment can over-sharpen already mastered tokens while amplifying learning pressure on uncertain, low-confidence tokens, leading to suboptimal training dynamics. We propose Trimmed Logit-Gap SFT (TrimSFT), a simple token-level reweighting method that scales the SFT loss according to the logit gap between the gold token and its strongest competitor. TrimSFT trims supervision away from both extremes: tokens already mastered (large logit gap) and tokens weakly supported by the current model (small or negative logit gap), concentrating learning within an intermediate logit-gap region between them. We instantiate this principle with a Gaussian weight centered at margin m with bandwidth τ, requiring no reference model or additional forward pass. We evaluate TrimSFT on six base models from the Llama, Qwen, and DeepMath families across five mathematical reasoning benchmarks. TrimSFT consistently improves over standard SFT, achieving the best average performance on five out of six models, with gains of up to +26.9 points over SFT on MATH500. Further analyses show that the bandwidth τ matters more than the exact margin location, and that half-trim variants that remove supervision pressure from only one side yield inferior trade-offs. A token-level logit-gap distribution analysis suggests that TrimSFT reshapes model confidence in a more balanced way than uniform SFT or monotonic reweighting methods. These results suggest that reasoning SFT can benefit from trimming both extremes rather than treating all tokens uniformly.

</details>


### [108] [Can Artificial Intelligence Support Healthcare and Mental Health Through Early Cyberbullying Detection ? The Impact of Emotion-Aware AI on Proactive Online Safety](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.09735)
*Hamed Jelodar,Amir Firouzi,Yen-Wu Lo,Maryam Tanha,Sajjad Dadkhah*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: CareGuard是一个早期预警框架，结合零样本语义标注和微调 transformer 模型(BERT、DistilBERT、RoBERTa)来检测网络欺凌相关内容，旨在支持医疗驱动的心理健康保护和主动在线安全。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 医疗系统、心理健康和公众福祉日益受到网络欺凌和有害在线互动的影响，需要一个能够早期预警并支持医疗心理健康保护的框架。

Method: 整合零样本语义标注与微调的transformer模型，并引入情感感知过滤机制和基于余弦相似度的语义筛选，以提高效率并减少不必要的计算。

Result: 在基准数据集上的实验表明，CareGuard能够有效平衡检测精度和计算效率，具有在医疗系统、心理健康监测和在线安全应用中规模化部署的潜力。

Conclusion: CareGuard框架为医疗场景下的网络欺凌检测提供了一个有效且可扩展的解决方案，有望在医疗心理健康监测领域得到应用。

Abstract: Healthcare systems, mental health, and public well-being are increasingly affected by cyberbullying and harmful online interactions. This paper presents CareGuard, an early-warning framework designed to support healthcare-driven mental health protection and proactive online safety through the detection of cyberbullying-related content using advanced natural language processing techniques.
